server/db.ts
import { Pool, neonConfig } from '@neondatabase/serverless';
import { drizzle } from 'drizzle-orm/neon-serverless';
import ws from "ws";
import * as schema from "@shared/schema";

neonConfig.webSocketConstructor = ws;

if (!process.env.DATABASE_URL) {
  throw new Error(
    "DATABASE_URL must be set. Did you forget to provision a database?",
  );
}

export const pool = new Pool({ connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL });
export const db = drizzle({ client: pool, schema });



server/index.ts
import express, { type Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { registerRoutes } from "./routes";
import { setupVite, serveStatic, log } from "./vite";

const app = express();
app.use(express.json());
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: false }));

app.use((req, res, next) => {
  const start = Date.now();
  const path = req.path;
  let capturedJsonResponse: Record<string, any> | undefined = undefined;

  const originalResJson = res.json;
  res.json = function (bodyJson, ...args) {
    capturedJsonResponse = bodyJson;
    return originalResJson.apply(res, [bodyJson, ...args]);
  };

  res.on("finish", () => {
    const duration = Date.now() - start;
    if (path.startsWith("/api")) {
      let logLine = `${req.method} ${path} ${res.statusCode} in ${duration}ms`;
      if (capturedJsonResponse) {
        logLine += ` :: ${JSON.stringify(capturedJsonResponse)}`;
      }

      if (logLine.length > 80) {
        logLine = logLine.slice(0, 79) + "…";
      }

      log(logLine);
    }
  });

  next();
});

(async () => {
  const server = await registerRoutes(app);

  app.use((err: any, _req: Request, res: Response, _next: NextFunction) => {
    const status = err.status || err.statusCode || 500;
    const message = err.message || "Internal Server Error";

    res.status(status).json({ message });
    throw err;
  });

  // importantly only setup vite in development and after
  // setting up all the other routes so the catch-all route
  // doesn't interfere with the other routes
  if (app.get("env") === "development") {
    await setupVite(app, server);
  } else {
    serveStatic(app);
  }

  // ALWAYS serve the app on port 5000
  // this serves both the API and the client.
  // It is the only port that is not firewalled.
  const port = 5000;
  server.listen({
    port,
    host: "0.0.0.0",
    reusePort: true,
  }, () => {
    log(`serving on port ${port}`);
  });
})();



server/routes-backup.ts
import type { Express } from "express";
import { createServer, type Server } from "http";
import { WebSocketServer, WebSocket } from "ws";
import { storage } from "./storage";
import { aiService } from "./services/aiService";
import multer from "multer";
import { z } from "zod";
import { insertUserSchema, insertConversationSchema, insertMessageSchema } from "@shared/schema";

// Configure multer for file uploads
const upload = multer({
  storage: multer.memoryStorage(),
  limits: {
    fileSize: 10 * 1024 * 1024, // 10MB
  },
  fileFilter: (req, file, cb) => {
    const allowedTypes = ['application/pdf', 'application/msword', 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document'];
    cb(null, allowedTypes.includes(file.mimetype));
  },
});

// Request schemas
const createUserSchema = insertUserSchema.extend({
  email: z.string().email(),
  name: z.string().min(1),
});

const chatMessageSchema = z.object({
  conversationId: z.number().optional(),
  message: z.string().min(1),
  userId: z.number(),
});

const documentAnalysisSchema = z.object({
  userId: z.number(),
});

const letterGenerationSchema = z.object({
  userId: z.number(),
  letterType: z.string(),
  context: z.string(),
});

export async function registerRoutes(app: Express): Promise<Server> {
  const httpServer = createServer(app);

  // WebSocket server for real-time chat
  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ server: httpServer, path: '/ws' });
  const clients = new Map<number, WebSocket>();

  wss.on('connection', (ws, req) => {
    const url = new URL(req.url!, `http://${req.headers.host}`);
    const userId = parseInt(url.searchParams.get('userId') || '0');
    
    if (userId > 0) {
      clients.set(userId, ws);
    }

    ws.on('close', () => {
      clients.delete(userId);
    });

    ws.on('message', (data) => {
      try {
        const message = JSON.parse(data.toString());
        if (message.type === 'typing') {
          // Broadcast typing indicator to other clients if needed
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('WebSocket message error:', error);
      }
    });
  });

  // User routes
  app.post('/api/users', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const userData = createUserSchema.parse(req.body);
      
      // Check if user exists
      const existingUser = await storage.getUserByEmail(userData.email);
      if (existingUser) {
        return res.status(400).json({ message: "User already exists" });
      }

      const user = await storage.createUser(userData);
      res.json(user);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/users/:id', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const user = await storage.getUser(id);
      
      if (!user) {
        return res.status(404).json({ message: "User not found" });
      }

      res.json(user);
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/users/:id/usage', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const usageCheck = await storage.checkUsageLimit(id);
      res.json(usageCheck);
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/users/:id/upgrade', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const user = await storage.updateUser(id, { 
        subscriptionPlan: "premium",
        usageLimit: 150 
      });
      
      if (!user) {
        return res.status(404).json({ message: "User not found" });
      }

      res.json(user);
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Conversation routes
  app.get('/api/conversations/:userId', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const userId = parseInt(req.params.userId);
      const conversations = await storage.getConversationsByUserId(userId);
      res.json(conversations);
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/conversations', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const conversationData = insertConversationSchema.parse(req.body);
      const conversation = await storage.createConversation(conversationData);
      res.json(conversation);
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.delete('/api/conversations/:id', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
      // In a real implementation, you'd add a delete method to storage
      res.json({ message: "Conversation deleted" });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/conversations/:id/messages', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const messages = await storage.getMessagesByConversationId(id);
      res.json(messages);
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Chat endpoint
  app.post('/api/chat', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { conversationId, message, userId } = chatMessageSchema.parse(req.body);
      
      let currentConversationId = conversationId;
      
      // Create new conversation if none exists
      if (!currentConversationId) {
        const user = await storage.getUser(userId);
        if (!user) {
          return res.status(404).json({ message: "User not found" });
        }
        
        const conversation = await storage.createConversation({
          userId,
          title: message.substring(0, 50) + "...",
          modelUsed: user.subscriptionPlan === "premium" ? "gpt-4o" : "mistral-small",
        });
        currentConversationId = conversation.id;
      }

      // Save user message
      await storage.createMessage({
        conversationId: currentConversationId,
        role: "user",
        content: message,
      });

      // Get conversation history
      const messages = await storage.getMessagesByConversationId(currentConversationId);
      const chatHistory = messages.map(msg => ({
        role: msg.role as "user" | "assistant" | "system",
        content: msg.content,
      }));

      // Process with AI
      const aiResponse = await aiService.processLegalQuestion(
        chatHistory,
        userId,
        currentConversationId
      );

      // Save AI response
      await storage.createMessage({
        conversationId: currentConversationId,
        role: "assistant",
        content: aiResponse.content,
        metadata: aiResponse.metadata,
      });

      // Send real-time update via WebSocket
      const userWs = clients.get(userId);
      if (userWs && userWs.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        userWs.send(JSON.stringify({
          type: "message",
          conversationId: currentConversationId,
          message: {
            role: "assistant",
            content: aiResponse.content,
            metadata: aiResponse.metadata,
          },
        }));
      }

      res.json({
        conversationId: currentConversationId,
        response: aiResponse.content,
        tokensUsed: aiResponse.tokensUsed,
        model: aiResponse.model,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Document analysis endpoint
  app.post('/api/analyze-document', upload.single('document'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      if (!req.file) {
        return res.status(400).json({ message: "No file uploaded" });
      }

      const { userId } = documentAnalysisSchema.parse(req.body);
      
      // Create document record
      const document = await storage.createDocument({
        userId,
        filename: req.file.originalname,
        fileType: req.file.mimetype,
        fileSize: req.file.size,
      });

      // Update status to processing
      await storage.updateDocument(document.id, { status: "processing" });

      try {
        // Convert buffer to text (simplified - in production, use proper PDF parsing)
        const fileContent = req.file.buffer.toString('utf-8');
        
        // Analyze with AI
        const aiResponse = await aiService.analyzeDocument(
          fileContent,
          req.file.originalname,
          userId
        );

        // Parse JSON response
        const analysisResult = JSON.parse(aiResponse.content);

        // Update document with results
        await storage.updateDocument(document.id, {
          status: "completed",
          analysisResult,
        });

        res.json({
          documentId: document.id,
          analysis: analysisResult,
          tokensUsed: aiResponse.tokensUsed,
          model: aiResponse.model,
        });
      } catch (error) {
        await storage.updateDocument(document.id, { status: "failed" });
        throw error;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Letter generation endpoint
  app.post('/api/generate-letter', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { userId, letterType, context } = letterGenerationSchema.parse(req.body);
      
      const aiResponse = await aiService.generateLetter(letterType, context, userId);
      
      res.json({
        letter: aiResponse.content,
        tokensUsed: aiResponse.tokensUsed,
        model: aiResponse.model,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Documents endpoint
  app.get('/api/documents/:userId', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const userId = parseInt(req.params.userId);
      const documents = await storage.getDocumentsByUserId(userId);
      res.json(documents);
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/documents/:id/analysis', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const document = await storage.getDocument(id);
      
      if (!document) {
        return res.status(404).json({ message: "Document not found" });
      }

      res.json({
        document,
        analysis: document.analysisResult,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Usage tracking endpoint
  app.get('/api/usage/:userId', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const userId = parseInt(req.params.userId);
      const startDate = req.query.start ? new Date(req.query.start as string) : undefined;
      const usage = await storage.getUsageByUserId(userId, startDate);
      res.json(usage);
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  return httpServer;
}



server/routes.ts
import type { Express } from "express";
import { createServer, type Server } from "http";
import { WebSocketServer, WebSocket } from "ws";
import multer from "multer";
import { z } from "zod";
import { storage } from "./storage";
import { aiService } from "./services/aiService";
import {
  insertUserSchema,
  insertConversationSchema,
  insertMessageSchema,
  insertDocumentSchema,
  insertUsageTrackingSchema,
} from "@shared/schema";

const upload = multer({ 
  storage: multer.memoryStorage(),
  limits: { fileSize: 10 * 1024 * 1024 }, // 10MB limit
});

const createUserSchema = insertUserSchema.extend({
  name: z.string().min(1, "Name is required"),
  email: z.string().email("Invalid email format"),
  subscriptionPlan: z.enum(["free", "premium"]).default("free"),
  usageLimit: z.number().default(50),
  usageCount: z.number().default(0),
});

const chatRequestSchema = z.object({
  userId: z.number(),
  conversationId: z.number().optional(),
  message: z.string().min(1, "Message is required"),
  model: z.string().optional(),
});

const documentAnalysisSchema = z.object({
  userId: z.number(),
  filename: z.string(),
  content: z.string(),
});

const letterGenerationSchema = z.object({
  userId: z.number(),
  letterType: z.string(),
  context: z.string(),
});

export async function registerRoutes(app: Express): Promise<Server> {
  const httpServer = createServer(app);

  // WebSocket server for real-time chat
  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ server: httpServer, path: '/ws' });
  const clients = new Map<number, WebSocket>();

  wss.on('connection', (ws, req) => {
    const url = new URL(req.url!, `http://${req.headers.host}`);
    const userId = parseInt(url.searchParams.get('userId') || '0');
    
    if (userId > 0) {
      clients.set(userId, ws);
    }

    ws.on('close', () => {
      clients.delete(userId);
    });

    ws.on('message', (data) => {
      try {
        const message = JSON.parse(data.toString());
        if (message.type === 'typing') {
          // Broadcast typing indicator to other clients if needed
        }
      } catch (error: any) {
        console.error('WebSocket message error:', error);
      }
    });
  });

  // User routes
  app.post('/api/users', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const userData = createUserSchema.parse(req.body);
      
      // Check if user exists
      const existingUser = await storage.getUserByEmail(userData.email);
      if (existingUser) {
        return res.status(400).json({ message: "User already exists" });
      }

      const user = await storage.createUser(userData);
      res.json(user);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/users/:id', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const user = await storage.getUser(id);
      
      if (!user) {
        return res.status(404).json({ message: "User not found" });
      }

      res.json(user);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/users/:id/usage', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const usageCheck = await storage.checkUsageLimit(id);
      res.json(usageCheck);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/users/:id/upgrade', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const user = await storage.updateUser(id, { 
        subscriptionPlan: "premium",
        usageLimit: 150 
      });
      
      if (!user) {
        return res.status(404).json({ message: "User not found" });
      }

      res.json(user);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Conversation routes
  app.get('/api/conversations/:userId', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const userId = parseInt(req.params.userId);
      const conversations = await storage.getConversationsByUserId(userId);
      res.json(conversations);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/conversations', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const conversationData = insertConversationSchema.parse(req.body);
      const conversation = await storage.createConversation(conversationData);
      res.json(conversation);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/conversations/:id/messages', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
      const messages = await storage.getMessagesByConversationId(id);
      res.json(messages);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Document routes
  app.get('/api/documents/:userId', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const userId = parseInt(req.params.userId);
      const documents = await storage.getDocumentsByUserId(userId);
      res.json(documents);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/documents/upload', upload.single('file'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      if (!req.file) {
        return res.status(400).json({ message: "No file uploaded" });
      }

      const { userId } = req.body;
      
      // Save document metadata
      const document = await storage.createDocument({
        userId: parseInt(userId),
        filename: req.file.originalname,
        fileType: req.file.mimetype,
        fileSize: req.file.size,
      });

      res.json(document);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // AI Chat endpoint
  app.post('/api/chat', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { userId, conversationId, message, model } = chatRequestSchema.parse(req.body);
      
      // Check usage limits
      const usageCheck = await storage.checkUsageLimit(userId);
      if (!usageCheck.canUse) {
        return res.status(429).json({ 
          message: "Usage limit exceeded",
          current: usageCheck.current,
          limit: usageCheck.limit
        });
      }

      // Get or create conversation
      let conversation;
      if (conversationId) {
        conversation = await storage.getConversation(conversationId);
      } else {
        conversation = await storage.createConversation({
          userId,
          title: message.substring(0, 50),
          modelUsed: model || "gpt-4o"
        });
      }

      if (!conversation) {
        return res.status(404).json({ message: "Conversation not found" });
      }

      // Save user message
      await storage.createMessage({
        conversationId: conversation.id,
        role: "user",
        content: message,
      });

      // Get AI response
      const aiResponse = await aiService.processLegalQuestion(message, model || "gpt-4o", userId);
      
      // Save AI response
      await storage.createMessage({
        conversationId: conversation.id,
        role: "assistant",
        content: aiResponse.content,
      });

      // Track usage
      await storage.incrementUsage(userId);
      await storage.createUsageTracking({
        userId,
        action: "chat",
        modelUsed: aiResponse.model,
        tokensUsed: aiResponse.tokensUsed,
        cost: 0.01, // Calculate based on model
      });

      // Send real-time update
      const userWs = clients.get(userId);
      if (userWs && userWs.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        userWs.send(JSON.stringify({
          type: "message",
          conversationId: conversation.id,
          message: {
            role: "assistant",
            content: aiResponse.content,
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
          }
        }));
      }

      res.json({
        conversationId: conversation.id,
        response: aiResponse.content,
        tokensUsed: aiResponse.tokensUsed,
        model: aiResponse.model,
      });
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Document analysis endpoint
  app.post('/api/analyze-document', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { userId, filename, content } = documentAnalysisSchema.parse(req.body);
      
      // Check usage limits
      const usageCheck = await storage.checkUsageLimit(userId);
      if (!usageCheck.canUse) {
        return res.status(429).json({ 
          message: "Usage limit exceeded",
          current: usageCheck.current,
          limit: usageCheck.limit
        });
      }

      // Analyze document
      const analysis = await aiService.analyzeDocument(content, filename, userId);
      
      // Update document status
      const documents = await storage.getDocumentsByUserId(userId);
      const document = documents.find(d => d.filename === filename);
      if (document) {
        await storage.updateDocument(document.id, {
          status: "completed",
          analysisResult: { content: analysis.content },
        });
      }

      // Track usage
      await storage.incrementUsage(userId);
      await storage.createUsageTracking({
        userId,
        action: "document_analysis",
        modelUsed: analysis.model,
        tokensUsed: analysis.tokensUsed,
        cost: 0.02,
      });

      res.json({
        analysis: analysis.content,
        tokensUsed: analysis.tokensUsed,
        model: analysis.model,
      });
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Letter generation endpoint
  app.post('/api/generate-letter', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { userId, letterType, context } = letterGenerationSchema.parse(req.body);
      
      // Check usage limits
      const usageCheck = await storage.checkUsageLimit(userId);
      if (!usageCheck.canUse) {
        return res.status(429).json({ 
          message: "Usage limit exceeded",
          current: usageCheck.current,
          limit: usageCheck.limit
        });
      }

      // Generate letter
      const letter = await aiService.generateLetter(letterType, context, "gpt-4o", userId);
      
      // Track usage
      await storage.incrementUsage(userId);
      await storage.createUsageTracking({
        userId,
        action: "letter_generation",
        modelUsed: letter.model,
        tokensUsed: letter.tokensUsed,
        cost: 3,
      });

      res.json({
        letter: letter.content,
        tokensUsed: letter.tokensUsed,
        model: letter.model,
      });
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  // Usage tracking endpoint
  app.get('/api/usage/:userId', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const userId = parseInt(req.params.userId);
      const { startDate } = req.query;
      
      const start = startDate ? new Date(startDate as string) : undefined;
      const usage = await storage.getUsageByUserId(userId, start);
      
      res.json(usage);
    } catch (error: any) {
      res.status(500).json({ message: error.message });
    }
  });

  return httpServer;
}



server/storage.ts
import {
  users,
  conversations,
  messages,
  documents,
  usageTracking,
  type User,
  type InsertUser,
  type Conversation,
  type InsertConversation,
  type Message,
  type InsertMessage,
  type Document,
  type InsertDocument,
  type UsageTracking,
  type InsertUsageTracking,
} from "@shared/schema";
import { db } from "./db";
import { eq, desc, and, gte, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

export interface IStorage {
  // User operations
  getUser(id: number): Promise<User | undefined>;
  getUserByEmail(email: string): Promise<User | undefined>;
  createUser(user: InsertUser): Promise<User>;
  updateUser(id: number, updates: Partial<User>): Promise<User | undefined>;
  
  // Conversation operations
  getConversation(id: number): Promise<Conversation | undefined>;
  getConversationsByUserId(userId: number): Promise<Conversation[]>;
  createConversation(conversation: InsertConversation): Promise<Conversation>;
  updateConversation(id: number, updates: Partial<Conversation>): Promise<Conversation | undefined>;
  
  // Message operations
  getMessagesByConversationId(conversationId: number): Promise<Message[]>;
  createMessage(message: InsertMessage): Promise<Message>;
  
  // Document operations
  getDocument(id: number): Promise<Document | undefined>;
  getDocumentsByUserId(userId: number): Promise<Document[]>;
  createDocument(document: InsertDocument): Promise<Document>;
  updateDocument(id: number, updates: Partial<Document>): Promise<Document | undefined>;
  
  // Usage tracking operations
  createUsageTracking(usage: InsertUsageTracking): Promise<UsageTracking>;
  getUsageByUserId(userId: number, startDate?: Date): Promise<UsageTracking[]>;
  
  // User limits and usage
  checkUsageLimit(userId: number): Promise<{ canUse: boolean; current: number; limit: number }>;
  incrementUsage(userId: number): Promise<void>;
  resetUsage(userId: number): Promise<void>;
}

export class DatabaseStorage implements IStorage {
  async getUser(id: number): Promise<User | undefined> {
    const [user] = await db.select().from(users).where(eq(users.id, id));
    return user;
  }

  async getUserByEmail(email: string): Promise<User | undefined> {
    const [user] = await db.select().from(users).where(eq(users.email, email));
    return user;
  }

  async createUser(insertUser: InsertUser): Promise<User> {
    const [user] = await db.insert(users).values(insertUser).returning();
    return user;
  }

  async updateUser(id: number, updates: Partial<User>): Promise<User | undefined> {
    const [user] = await db
      .update(users)
      .set({ ...updates, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(users.id, id))
      .returning();
    return user;
  }

  async getConversation(id: number): Promise<Conversation | undefined> {
    const [conversation] = await db.select().from(conversations).where(eq(conversations.id, id));
    return conversation;
  }

  async getConversationsByUserId(userId: number): Promise<Conversation[]> {
    return await db
      .select()
      .from(conversations)
      .where(eq(conversations.userId, userId))
      .orderBy(desc(conversations.updatedAt));
  }

  async createConversation(insertConversation: InsertConversation): Promise<Conversation> {
    const [conversation] = await db.insert(conversations).values(insertConversation).returning();
    return conversation;
  }

  async updateConversation(id: number, updates: Partial<Conversation>): Promise<Conversation | undefined> {
    const [conversation] = await db
      .update(conversations)
      .set({ ...updates, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(conversations.id, id))
      .returning();
    return conversation;
  }

  async getMessagesByConversationId(conversationId: number): Promise<Message[]> {
    return await db
      .select()
      .from(messages)
      .where(eq(messages.conversationId, conversationId))
      .orderBy(messages.createdAt);
  }

  async createMessage(insertMessage: InsertMessage): Promise<Message> {
    const [message] = await db.insert(messages).values(insertMessage).returning();
    return message;
  }

  async getDocument(id: number): Promise<Document | undefined> {
    const [document] = await db.select().from(documents).where(eq(documents.id, id));
    return document;
  }

  async getDocumentsByUserId(userId: number): Promise<Document[]> {
    return await db
      .select()
      .from(documents)
      .where(eq(documents.userId, userId))
      .orderBy(desc(documents.createdAt));
  }

  async createDocument(insertDocument: InsertDocument): Promise<Document> {
    const [document] = await db.insert(documents).values(insertDocument).returning();
    return document;
  }

  async updateDocument(id: number, updates: Partial<Document>): Promise<Document | undefined> {
    const [document] = await db
      .update(documents)
      .set({ ...updates, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(documents.id, id))
      .returning();
    return document;
  }

  async createUsageTracking(insertUsage: InsertUsageTracking): Promise<UsageTracking> {
    const [usage] = await db.insert(usageTracking).values(insertUsage).returning();
    return usage;
  }

  async getUsageByUserId(userId: number, startDate?: Date): Promise<UsageTracking[]> {
    let query = db.select().from(usageTracking).where(eq(usageTracking.userId, userId));
    
    if (startDate) {
      query = db.select().from(usageTracking).where(and(eq(usageTracking.userId, userId), gte(usageTracking.timestamp, startDate)));
    }
    
    return await query.orderBy(desc(usageTracking.timestamp));
  }

  async checkUsageLimit(userId: number): Promise<{ canUse: boolean; current: number; limit: number }> {
    const user = await this.getUser(userId);
    if (!user) {
      throw new Error("User not found");
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const resetDate = new Date(user.resetDate || now);
    
    // Check if we need to reset usage (monthly reset)
    if (now >= resetDate) {
      await this.resetUsage(userId);
      return { canUse: true, current: 0, limit: user.usageLimit };
    }

    return {
      canUse: user.usageCount < user.usageLimit,
      current: user.usageCount,
      limit: user.usageLimit,
    };
  }

  async incrementUsage(userId: number): Promise<void> {
    await db
      .update(users)
      .set({ 
        usageCount: sql`${users.usageCount} + 1`,
        updatedAt: new Date()
      })
      .where(eq(users.id, userId));
  }

  async resetUsage(userId: number): Promise<void> {
    const nextMonth = new Date();
    nextMonth.setMonth(nextMonth.getMonth() + 1);
    
    await db
      .update(users)
      .set({ 
        usageCount: 0,
        resetDate: nextMonth,
        updatedAt: new Date()
      })
      .where(eq(users.id, userId));
  }
}

export const storage = new DatabaseStorage();



server/vite.ts
import express, { type Express } from "express";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { createServer as createViteServer, createLogger } from "vite";
import { type Server } from "http";
import viteConfig from "../vite.config";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

const viteLogger = createLogger();

export function log(message: string, source = "express") {
  const formattedTime = new Date().toLocaleTimeString("en-US", {
    hour: "numeric",
    minute: "2-digit",
    second: "2-digit",
    hour12: true,
  });

  console.log(`${formattedTime} [${source}] ${message}`);
}

export async function setupVite(app: Express, server: Server) {
  const serverOptions = {
    middlewareMode: true,
    hmr: { server },
    allowedHosts: true as const,
  };

  const vite = await createViteServer({
    ...viteConfig,
    configFile: false,
    customLogger: {
      ...viteLogger,
      error: (msg, options) => {
        viteLogger.error(msg, options);
        process.exit(1);
      },
    },
    server: serverOptions,
    appType: "custom",
  });

  app.use(vite.middlewares);
  app.use("*", async (req, res, next) => {
    const url = req.originalUrl;

    try {
      const clientTemplate = path.resolve(
        import.meta.dirname,
        "..",
        "client",
        "index.html",
      );

      // always reload the index.html file from disk incase it changes
      let template = await fs.promises.readFile(clientTemplate, "utf-8");
      template = template.replace(
        `src="/src/main.tsx"`,
        `src="/src/main.tsx?v=${nanoid()}"`,
      );
      const page = await vite.transformIndexHtml(url, template);
      res.status(200).set({ "Content-Type": "text/html" }).end(page);
    } catch (e) {
      vite.ssrFixStacktrace(e as Error);
      next(e);
    }
  });
}

export function serveStatic(app: Express) {
  const distPath = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "public");

  if (!fs.existsSync(distPath)) {
    throw new Error(
      `Could not find the build directory: ${distPath}, make sure to build the client first`,
    );
  }

  app.use(express.static(distPath));

  // fall through to index.html if the file doesn't exist
  app.use("*", (_req, res) => {
    res.sendFile(path.resolve(distPath, "index.html"));
  });
}



server/services/aiService.ts
import { OpenAIService } from "./openaiService";
import { OpenRouterService } from "./openrouterService";
import { storage } from "../storage";

export interface AIResponse {
  content: string;
  tokensUsed: number;
  model: string;
  analysisType?: string;
  metadata?: any;
}

export interface ChatMessage {
  role: "user" | "assistant" | "system";
  content: string;
}

export class AIService {
  private openaiService: OpenAIService;
  private openrouterService: OpenRouterService;

  constructor() {
    this.openaiService = new OpenAIService();
    this.openrouterService = new OpenRouterService();
  }

  async processLegalQuestion(
    message: string,
    model?: string,
    userId?: number
  ): Promise<AIResponse> {
    // For backward compatibility, create a simple message structure
    const messages: ChatMessage[] = [
      {
        role: "user",
        content: message
      }
    ];

    // Use default model if not specified
    const selectedModel = model || "gpt-4o";
    
    // Add legal context to system message
    const systemMessage: ChatMessage = {
      role: "system",
      content: `You are LexAI, a revolutionary AI legal assistant. You provide accurate, helpful legal information while being clear that you're not providing legal advice but legal education.

Key principles:
- Explain laws in simple, accessible language
- Protect users from intimidation and false authority
- Provide calm, confident guidance
- Reference relevant legal articles and statutes when possible
- Always emphasize user rights and proper procedures
- If someone mentions threats or intimidation, provide supportive guidance
- Format responses clearly with sections for: Legal Analysis, Your Rights, Recommended Actions

Remember: You're helping eliminate fear of the law by making legal information accessible to everyone.`
    };

    const fullMessages = [systemMessage, ...messages];

    try {
      const isPremium = selectedModel === "gpt-4o";
      const aiService = isPremium ? this.openaiService : this.openrouterService;
      
      const response = await aiService.chat(fullMessages, selectedModel);
      
      return response;
    } catch (error: any) {
      throw new Error(`AI service error: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  async processLegalQuestionWithContext(
    messages: ChatMessage[],
    userId: number,
    conversationId: number
  ): Promise<AIResponse> {
    // Check usage limits
    const usageCheck = await storage.checkUsageLimit(userId);
    if (!usageCheck.canUse) {
      throw new Error(`You've reached your ${usageCheck.limit} question limit this month. Upgrade to Premium or wait for the new cycle.`);
    }

    // Get user subscription plan
    const user = await storage.getUser(userId);
    if (!user) {
      throw new Error("User not found");
    }

    const isPremium = user.subscriptionPlan === "premium";
    
    // Choose AI service based on subscription
    const aiService = isPremium ? this.openaiService : this.openrouterService;
    const model = isPremium ? "gpt-4o" : "mistral-small";

    // Add legal context to system message
    const systemMessage: ChatMessage = {
      role: "system",
      content: `You are LexAI, a revolutionary AI legal assistant. You provide accurate, helpful legal information while being clear that you're not providing legal advice but legal education.

Key principles:
- Explain laws in simple, accessible language
- Protect users from intimidation and false authority
- Provide calm, confident guidance
- Reference relevant legal articles and statutes when possible
- Always emphasize user rights and proper procedures
- If someone mentions threats or intimidation, provide supportive guidance
- Format responses clearly with sections for: Legal Analysis, Your Rights, Recommended Actions

Remember: You're helping eliminate fear of the law by making legal information accessible to everyone.`
    };

    const fullMessages = [systemMessage, ...messages];

    try {
      const response = await aiService.chat(fullMessages, model);
      
      // Track usage
      await storage.incrementUsage(userId);
      await storage.createUsageTracking({
        userId,
        action: "question",
        modelUsed: model,
        tokensUsed: response.tokensUsed,
        cost: this.calculateCost(response.tokensUsed, model),
      });

      return response;
    } catch (error) {
      throw new Error(`AI service error: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  async analyzeDocument(
    fileContent: string,
    fileName: string,
    userId?: number
  ): Promise<AIResponse> {
    // Check usage limits
    const usageCheck = await storage.checkUsageLimit(userId);
    if (!usageCheck.canUse) {
      throw new Error(`You've reached your ${usageCheck.limit} question limit this month. Upgrade to Premium or wait for the new cycle.`);
    }

    // Get user subscription plan
    const user = await storage.getUser(userId);
    if (!user) {
      throw new Error("User not found");
    }

    const isPremium = user.subscriptionPlan === "premium";
    
    if (!isPremium) {
      throw new Error("Document analysis is only available with Premium subscription. Upgrade to LexAI Elite for full legal power.");
    }

    const messages: ChatMessage[] = [
      {
        role: "system",
        content: `You are LexAI's document analysis expert. Analyze legal documents with precision and provide structured insights.

Provide your analysis in JSON format with these sections:
- keyInsights: Array of important findings
- riskAssessment: {level: "low|medium|high", score: 0-100, concerns: []}
- recommendations: Array of actionable suggestions
- summary: Brief overview of the document
- legalIssues: Array of potential legal concerns

Focus on practical implications for the user.`
      },
      {
        role: "user",
        content: `Please analyze this legal document (${fileName}):\n\n${fileContent}`
      }
    ];

    try {
      const response = await this.openaiService.chat(messages, "gpt-4o", { 
        response_format: { type: "json_object" } 
      });
      
      // Track usage
      await storage.incrementUsage(userId);
      await storage.createUsageTracking({
        userId,
        action: "document_analysis",
        modelUsed: "gpt-4o",
        tokensUsed: response.tokensUsed,
        cost: this.calculateCost(response.tokensUsed, "gpt-4o"),
      });

      return {
        ...response,
        analysisType: "document",
        metadata: { fileName, fileSize: fileContent.length }
      };
    } catch (error) {
      throw new Error(`Document analysis error: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  async generateLetter(
    letterType: string,
    context: string,
    model?: string,
    userId?: number
  ): Promise<AIResponse> {
    // Check usage limits and premium status
    const usageCheck = await storage.checkUsageLimit(userId);
    if (!usageCheck.canUse) {
      throw new Error(`You've reached your ${usageCheck.limit} question limit this month. Upgrade to Premium or wait for the new cycle.`);
    }

    const user = await storage.getUser(userId);
    if (!user) {
      throw new Error("User not found");
    }

    const isPremium = user.subscriptionPlan === "premium";
    
    if (!isPremium) {
      throw new Error("Letter generation is only available with Premium subscription. Upgrade to LexAI Elite for full legal power.");
    }

    const messages: ChatMessage[] = [
      {
        role: "system",
        content: `You are LexAI's legal letter generation specialist. Create professional, legally sound letters and documents.

Generate formal legal correspondence that is:
- Professional and appropriately formal
- Legally accurate and properly formatted
- Clear and assertive without being aggressive
- Properly structured with date, addresses, subject, body, and closing
- Includes relevant legal references when appropriate

Always include placeholder fields for personalization like [Your Name], [Date], [Recipient Name], etc.`
      },
      {
        role: "user",
        content: `Please generate a ${letterType} letter with the following context:\n\n${context}`
      }
    ];

    try {
      const response = await this.openaiService.chat(messages, "gpt-4o");
      
      // Track usage
      await storage.incrementUsage(userId);
      await storage.createUsageTracking({
        userId,
        action: "letter_generation",
        modelUsed: "gpt-4o",
        tokensUsed: response.tokensUsed,
        cost: this.calculateCost(response.tokensUsed, "gpt-4o"),
      });

      return {
        ...response,
        analysisType: "letter",
        metadata: { letterType, context }
      };
    } catch (error) {
      throw new Error(`Letter generation error: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  private calculateCost(tokens: number, model: string): number {
    // Cost in cents
    const rates = {
      "gpt-4o": 0.005, // $0.005 per 1k tokens
      "mistral-small": 0.001, // $0.001 per 1k tokens
    };
    
    const rate = rates[model] || 0.001;
    return Math.round((tokens / 1000) * rate * 100); // Convert to cents
  }
}

export const aiService = new AIService();



server/service/openaiService.ts
import OpenAI from "openai";

export interface ChatMessage {
  role: "user" | "assistant" | "system";
  content: string;
}

export interface AIResponse {
  content: string;
  tokensUsed: number;
  model: string;
}

export class OpenAIService {
  private openai: OpenAI;

  constructor() {
    const apiKey = process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY || process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY_ENV_VAR;
    if (!apiKey) {
      throw new Error("OpenAI API key is required");
    }
    
    this.openai = new OpenAI({ apiKey });
  }

  async chat(messages: ChatMessage[], model: string = "gpt-4o", options?: any): Promise<AIResponse> {
    try {
      // the newest OpenAI model is "gpt-4o" which was released May 13, 2024. do not change this unless explicitly requested by the user
      const response = await this.openai.chat.completions.create({
        model,
        messages,
        temperature: 0.7,
        max_tokens: 2000,
        ...options,
      });

      const choice = response.choices[0];
      if (!choice?.message?.content) {
        throw new Error("No response from OpenAI");
      }

      return {
        content: choice.message.content,
        tokensUsed: response.usage?.total_tokens || 0,
        model,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("OpenAI API error:", error);
      throw new Error(`OpenAI API error: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  async analyzeDocument(base64Content: string, fileName: string): Promise<AIResponse> {
    try {
      const response = await this.openai.chat.completions.create({
        model: "gpt-4o",
        messages: [
          {
            role: "system",
            content: "You are a legal document analysis expert. Analyze the document and provide structured insights in JSON format."
          },
          {
            role: "user",
            content: [
              {
                type: "text",
                text: `Please analyze this legal document (${fileName}) and provide insights on key clauses, risks, and recommendations.`
              },
              {
                type: "image_url",
                image_url: {
                  url: `data:application/pdf;base64,${base64Content}`
                }
              }
            ]
          }
        ],
        max_tokens: 2000,
        response_format: { type: "json_object" },
      });

      const choice = response.choices[0];
      if (!choice?.message?.content) {
        throw new Error("No response from OpenAI");
      }

      return {
        content: choice.message.content,
        tokensUsed: response.usage?.total_tokens || 0,
        model: "gpt-4o",
      };
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("OpenAI document analysis error:", error);
      throw new Error(`OpenAI document analysis error: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }
}



server/services/openrouterService.ts
export interface ChatMessage {
  role: "user" | "assistant" | "system";
  content: string;
}

export interface AIResponse {
  content: string;
  tokensUsed: number;
  model: string;
}

export class OpenRouterService {
  private apiKey: string;
  private baseUrl: string = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1";

  constructor() {
    this.apiKey = process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY || process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY_ENV_VAR;
    if (!this.apiKey) {
      throw new Error("OpenRouter API key is required");
    }
  }

  async chat(messages: ChatMessage[], model: string = "mistralai/mistral-small-24b-instruct-2501:free"): Promise<AIResponse> {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/chat/completions`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Authorization": `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          "HTTP-Referer": "https://lexai.app", // Required for OpenRouter
          "X-Title": "LexAI", // Required for OpenRouter
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model,
          messages,
          temperature: 0.7,
          max_tokens: 2000,
        }),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const error = await response.text();
        throw new Error(`OpenRouter API error: ${error}`);
      }

      const data = await response.json();
      const choice = data.choices[0];
      
      if (!choice?.message?.content) {
        throw new Error("No response from OpenRouter");
      }

      return {
        content: choice.message.content,
        tokensUsed: data.usage?.total_tokens || 0,
        model,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("OpenRouter API error:", error);
      throw new Error(`OpenRouter API error: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }
}



shared/schema.ts
import { pgTable, text, serial, integer, boolean, timestamp, jsonb, varchar, uuid } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
import { createInsertSchema } from "drizzle-zod";
import { z } from "zod";
import { relations } from "drizzle-orm";

// Users table
export const users = pgTable("users", {
  id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
  email: text("email").notNull().unique(),
  name: text("name").notNull(),
  subscriptionPlan: text("subscription_plan").notNull().default("free"), // free, premium
  usageCount: integer("usage_count").notNull().default(0),
  usageLimit: integer("usage_limit").notNull().default(50),
  resetDate: timestamp("reset_date").defaultNow(),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at").defaultNow(),
});

// Conversations table
export const conversations = pgTable("conversations", {
  id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
  userId: integer("user_id").references(() => users.id).notNull(),
  title: text("title").notNull(),
  modelUsed: text("model_used").notNull(), // gpt-4o, mistral-small
  tokensUsed: integer("tokens_used").default(0),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at").defaultNow(),
});

// Messages table
export const messages = pgTable("messages", {
  id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
  conversationId: integer("conversation_id").references(() => conversations.id).notNull(),
  role: text("role").notNull(), // user, assistant, system
  content: text("content").notNull(),
  metadata: jsonb("metadata"), // For storing additional info like document analysis results
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow(),
});

// Documents table for analysis
export const documents = pgTable("documents", {
  id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
  userId: integer("user_id").references(() => users.id).notNull(),
  filename: text("filename").notNull(),
  fileType: text("file_type").notNull(),
  fileSize: integer("file_size").notNull(),
  analysisResult: jsonb("analysis_result"),
  status: text("status").notNull().default("pending"), // pending, processing, completed, failed
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at").defaultNow(),
});

// Usage tracking table
export const usageTracking = pgTable("usage_tracking", {
  id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
  userId: integer("user_id").references(() => users.id).notNull(),
  action: text("action").notNull(), // question, document_analysis, letter_generation
  modelUsed: text("model_used").notNull(),
  tokensUsed: integer("tokens_used").default(0),
  cost: integer("cost").default(0), // in cents
  timestamp: timestamp("timestamp").defaultNow(),
});

// Relations
export const usersRelations = relations(users, ({ many }) => ({
  conversations: many(conversations),
  documents: many(documents),
  usageTracking: many(usageTracking),
}));

export const conversationsRelations = relations(conversations, ({ one, many }) => ({
  user: one(users, { fields: [conversations.userId], references: [users.id] }),
  messages: many(messages),
}));

export const messagesRelations = relations(messages, ({ one }) => ({
  conversation: one(conversations, { fields: [messages.conversationId], references: [conversations.id] }),
}));

export const documentsRelations = relations(documents, ({ one }) => ({
  user: one(users, { fields: [documents.userId], references: [users.id] }),
}));

export const usageTrackingRelations = relations(usageTracking, ({ one }) => ({
  user: one(users, { fields: [usageTracking.userId], references: [users.id] }),
}));

// Zod schemas
export const insertUserSchema = createInsertSchema(users).pick({
  email: true,
  name: true,
  subscriptionPlan: true,
});

export const insertConversationSchema = createInsertSchema(conversations).pick({
  userId: true,
  title: true,
  modelUsed: true,
});

export const insertMessageSchema = createInsertSchema(messages).pick({
  conversationId: true,
  role: true,
  content: true,
  metadata: true,
});

export const insertDocumentSchema = createInsertSchema(documents).pick({
  userId: true,
  filename: true,
  fileType: true,
  fileSize: true,
});

export const insertUsageTrackingSchema = createInsertSchema(usageTracking).pick({
  userId: true,
  action: true,
  modelUsed: true,
  tokensUsed: true,
  cost: true,
});

// Types
export type User = typeof users.$inferSelect;
export type InsertUser = z.infer<typeof insertUserSchema>;
export type Conversation = typeof conversations.$inferSelect;
export type InsertConversation = z.infer<typeof insertConversationSchema>;
export type Message = typeof messages.$inferSelect;
export type InsertMessage = z.infer<typeof insertMessageSchema>;
export type Document = typeof documents.$inferSelect;
export type InsertDocument = z.infer<typeof insertDocumentSchema>;
export type UsageTracking = typeof usageTracking.$inferSelect;
export type InsertUsageTracking = z.infer<typeof insertUsageTrackingSchema>;

replit.md
# LexAI - AI Legal Assistant

## Overview

LexAI is a revolutionary AI-powered legal assistant application built as a full-stack web application. It provides users with accessible legal advice, document analysis, and legal protection through an intuitive chat interface. The application features a dual-model AI strategy with free and premium tiers, offering different AI capabilities based on subscription plans.

## User Preferences

Preferred communication style: Simple, everyday language.

## System Architecture

### Frontend Architecture
- **Framework**: React 18 with TypeScript
- **Styling**: TailwindCSS with CSS variables for theming
- **UI Components**: shadcn/ui component library with Radix UI primitives
- **State Management**: TanStack Query (React Query) for server state management
- **Routing**: Wouter for client-side routing
- **Build Tool**: Vite for development and build processes

### Backend Architecture
- **Runtime**: Node.js with Express.js framework
- **Language**: TypeScript with ES modules
- **API**: RESTful API with real-time WebSocket support
- **File Handling**: Multer for document uploads (PDF, Word documents)
- **Validation**: Zod for request validation and schema enforcement

### Database & ORM
- **Database**: PostgreSQL (configured for Neon serverless)
- **ORM**: Drizzle ORM with schema-first approach
- **Connection**: Connection pooling with @neondatabase/serverless
- **Migrations**: Drizzle Kit for schema management

## Key Components

### AI Service Layer
- **Dual-Model Strategy**: 
  - Free tier: Mistral Small via OpenRouter API
  - Premium tier: GPT-4o via OpenAI API
- **Usage Tracking**: Token-based usage limits with monthly reset cycles
- **Service Abstraction**: Unified interface for different AI providers

### Authentication & User Management
- **User System**: Custom user management with subscription plans
- **Usage Limits**: Monthly question limits (50 for free, 150 for premium)
- **Plan Management**: Free and Premium subscription tiers

### Real-time Features
- **WebSocket Integration**: Real-time chat functionality
- **Live Updates**: Real-time conversation updates and typing indicators
- **Connection Management**: WebSocket client mapping for user sessions

### Document Processing
- **File Upload**: Support for PDF and Word documents
- **Analysis Pipeline**: AI-powered document analysis and insights
- **Storage**: File metadata and analysis results stored in database

## Data Flow

### Chat Flow
1. User sends message through chat interface
2. Usage limits checked against user subscription
3. Message routed to appropriate AI service (OpenAI or OpenRouter)
4. AI response processed and stored in database
5. Real-time response delivered via WebSocket
6. Usage tracking updated

### Document Analysis Flow
1. User uploads document via drag-and-drop interface
2. File validated and stored temporarily
3. Document content extracted and analyzed by AI
4. Analysis results structured and stored
5. Insights presented to user through UI

### User Management Flow
1. User registration/login handled through custom system
2. Subscription status determines AI model access
3. Usage tracking prevents over-limit requests
4. Monthly reset cycles managed automatically

## External Dependencies

### AI Services
- **OpenAI API**: GPT-4o for premium users
- **OpenRouter API**: Mistral Small for free users
- **Usage Tracking**: Token consumption monitoring

### Database
- **Neon PostgreSQL**: Serverless PostgreSQL hosting
- **Connection Pooling**: Efficient database connection management

### Development Tools
- **Replit Integration**: Development environment support
- **Vite Plugins**: Development experience enhancements
- **TypeScript**: Type safety across the stack

## Deployment Strategy

### Development Environment
- **Local Development**: Vite dev server with hot module replacement
- **Database**: Local PostgreSQL or Neon development database
- **Environment Variables**: Separate configs for development/production

### Production Build
- **Frontend**: Vite build with optimized bundles
- **Backend**: esbuild compilation for Node.js deployment
- **Static Assets**: Served through Express static middleware

### Environment Configuration
- **API Keys**: OpenAI and OpenRouter API keys required
- **Database**: PostgreSQL connection string (DATABASE_URL)
- **Build Process**: Separate build steps for client and server

### Scalability Considerations
- **Database**: Drizzle ORM with connection pooling
- **Real-time**: WebSocket server scaling considerations
- **AI Services**: Rate limiting and error handling for external APIs

The application follows a modern full-stack architecture with clear separation of concerns, type safety throughout, and scalable patterns for growth. The dual-AI strategy allows for monetization while providing value to free users, and the real-time features enhance user experience.

vite.config.ts
import { defineConfig } from "vite";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";
import path from "path";
import runtimeErrorOverlay from "@replit/vite-plugin-runtime-error-modal";

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [
    react(),
    runtimeErrorOverlay(),
    ...(process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production" &&
    process.env.REPL_ID !== undefined
      ? [
          await import("@replit/vite-plugin-cartographer").then((m) =>
            m.cartographer(),
          ),
        ]
      : []),
  ],
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      "@": path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "client", "src"),
      "@shared": path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "shared"),
      "@assets": path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "attached_assets"),
    },
  },
  root: path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "client"),
  build: {
    outDir: path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "dist/public"),
    emptyOutDir: true,
  },
  server: {
    fs: {
      strict: true,
      deny: ["**/.*"],
    },
  },
});
